  Read literally ("+0.185 W per image") both would fail, −0.068 and −0.215 W.
- **C1 PASS:** E139's own images `E56` and `E448` read +0.199 and +0.169 W against +0.213 (−0.013, −0.044 W).
- **N2 A ONLY:** the 32-bit count and the pins are excluded at every arm (counting at 32 bits would miss `E32` by −0.486 W), the
  line edge on the primary; the 128-bit addition only on this card's primary and leakage arms, by one pair (`E192`, −0.117
  [−0.198, −0.035] W), so 64 bits is the reading on present evidence and 64 + 128 is not excluded across cards (C3).
- **D EVIDENT:** 192 pins held high cost +0.397 W [0.326, 0.468].
- **No card term is resolved:** the pins read 18–29% higher on card 1, p 0.10 and 0.06 (C2); the encoded price fitted on card 1
  alone 0.389 [0.286, 0.493] pJ against 0.463.
- Post hoc (O1): 10 of the 17 in-window dips fell on the encoded-quiet twins (p 0.007), so the dips-kept arm is biased pair by
  pair.

**Scored:** N1 PASS; C1 PASS; N2 A ONLY; D EVIDENT.
**What it changes.** Item 10: the encoded term carries to a second card and two new images, its out-of-sample error 0.144 W rms
with no term → 0.033 W on the four scored pairs; it is counted at 64 bits on present evidence, and if 128-bit toggles add, random
data's term at full read rate is 1.86 times as large (+0.11 W). Open: the 1.0 pJ split between the memory shires' logic, the PHY
and the DRAM; where the 256-bit path is; a second card's `E192`.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

## E157 — blind pool 6 on aifoundry1's card 1: M8 on the second 600 MHz card, every launch's C checked, the card term (Q139; card runs 2026-11-03, 16:20:11–17:06:21 PST, aifoundry1's card 1) — pre-registered

**Asked:** item 6 of section 11, and Q138's final critic's ranks 2, 14 and 5, as E153 (above), on card 1. **Pre-registered** in
[`tools/claims-v3/e157/README.md`](../../tools/claims-v3/e157/README.md) (`01676499`, committed and pushed 16:17:57 PST;
`d98d2e34` its ancestor), before the claim (16:19:48, the evening's day post, which this lane created) and the first launch
(16:20:31). Data: [`docs/reports/data/2026-11-03-e157-blind6-card1/`](../reports/data/2026-11-03-e157-blind6-card1/README.md)
(`fb2e5c6e`; the card term scored on E153's committed reduction in `80420324`, wording `a3823707`; corrected after verification in
`7931262e` and `26096a60`, C1–C5 and C2′, beside a dated note on the registration; no number or verdict changed). aifoundry1's
card 1 at 600 MHz in all 27,115 samples, TDP 65 W / 65 °C, 499 mV in all 14 config reads, never set; **46.2 card-minutes** of 55:
the stop-path test 15702, the smoke 15701 and four passes, 15710–15713, three kept (15711 set aside by E78's pass gate,
`fp16.zeros` +0.384 W off its line). Reads only; the board at most 61.10 W (stop 66), the shire mean 63 °C (stop 72).

**Results** (`scored.json` › `primary`: Q139(u)'s footing, 0.3134 W/°C with A2 once).
- **Every launch's C bit-exact:** 3,170 of 3,170 (fp32 1,226, fp16 852, int8 1,092): the first checked reads on card 1 of int8's
  wrap, full-range int8, wide fp16 exponents and real tiles; E160 read those ranges first, on aifoundry2 (C2).
- **K1 FAIL:** the six pool-5 tiles came back within Welch's interval of E142's on 5 of 6 and within 0.05 W on 3 (`int8.gray_a`
  −0.187 W, outside ±0.152); E78's random anchors moved at most 0.061 W. Repeat D between days on card 1 reproduces to 0.1–0.2 W.
- **Power:** M8 0.386 / 0.098 / 0.327 W rms (fp32 / fp16 / int8; P1 FAIL, its line 0.302), 0.323 W over the 30 priority-1 tiles
  (bias −0.012 W), 0.366 W over the 44 new kinds; the floats' bias **+0.002 W** against M6's −0.127 W on the same 18 tiles; **29
  of 30** inside (`fp32.b_onebit_b20_third` outside, 1.09 W under every model) *(M8's 1.09 W; M7 1.21, M6 1.12 W, E157's table; Q140(c)'s fix pass, Wed 4 Nov 2026)*.
- **Not better than M6:** M8 − M6 +0.040 W [−0.013, +0.128], NOT RESOLVED (+0.086 [+0.021, +0.178], WORSE, on E142's own footing);
  M6 0.377 / 0.134 / 0.222 W.
- **By case** (post hoc, C1): the two seeded-weight tiles 0.082 W, the two C-register tiles 0.094 W (M6 0.322: the C-register term
  works where aimed), the 12 real tiles 0.146 W with 12 of 12 inside (the best-read of the cases with more than two tiles),
  whole-line int8 0.717 W (the near-whole-byte discount over-corrects: `int8.b_line_55_aa` +1.36, `int8.a_zeros_b_lineflip` +0.78
  W).
- **Heat:** 0.180 / 0.183 / 0.123 °C rms, 29 of 30 inside (H1–H4 PASS); 0.1160 °C per board W against 0.1151.
- **The card term (rank 5)**, scored at 17:27 after E153's commit with the registered code: X1 PASS, card 1's float D over
  aifoundry3's on 25 tiles **0.9632 [0.9613, 0.9659]** against M8's 0.9616 (M7's 0.9711 outside); X2 PASS, int8 0.9681 [0.9534,
  0.9783]; S1 and S2 PASS, the frozen zeros-level read predicting the scale to +0.78 / −0.74% (V² −5.5 / −6.9%). On the registered
  footing only: the ratio moves 0.954–0.973 across card 1's arms (X1 FAIL on 3 of 5, S1 on 2), and the zeros level re-read on the
  day is +10% off.

**Scored:** the check of C (3,170 of 3,170) and K1-C PASS; P2, P3, B1, B2, C1, H1–H4, R1 and R2 PASS; X1, X2, S1 and S2 PASS,
scored on E153's reduction; P1 FAIL; K1 FAIL (K1-D, K1-W); M1 and M2 NOT RESOLVED.
**What it changes.** Item 6 on card 1: M8's refit did not lower the blind error; it removed the float bias on its footing while
its scatter grew on whole-line int8 and exponent-heavy fp32. Carrying a prediction from aifoundry3 to card 1 costs under 1% on the
one footing. Rank 14's C half is closed on card 1; its D half fails as written (0.1–0.2 W between days, not 0.05). Named for M9,
not fitted: cap or drop the whole-byte discount, an fp32 exponent-field term, int8 held-B, B's high mantissa bits.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

## E158 — a large GEMM's time per op as the L3's retention by reuse distance (Q139; card runs 2026-11-03, 04:39:07–04:59:57 PST, aifoundry2) — pre-registered

**Asked:** item 1 of section 11, and rank 7 of Q138's final critic: *"Register a ladder of B-panel reuse distances from 20 to 56
MB, with and without C's traffic, with MAB's frozen prices predicting the L3-served share. Rule: the fitted retention curve
predicts F6's time per op within 3%."* **Pre-registered** in [`tools/claims-v3/e158/`](../../tools/claims-v3/e158/E158.md)
(`2cfc4525`, committed 04:37:54 PST, on origin 04:37:56), before the claim (04:38:25) and the first card touch (04:39:07). Data:
[`docs/reports/data/2026-11-03-e158-l3-retention/`](../reports/data/2026-11-03-e158-l3-retention/README.md) (`f8272c64`; corrected
after verification in `bc89f1bc`, C1–C5; no frozen text, number or verdict changed). aifoundry2's card 0 under its own governor,
65 W / 65 °C (low_power, 600 MHz, 519 mV) at the start, the end and after every run, nothing set; `dmcmd_audit` GO (19 READ
sites); **20.8 card-minutes**: 98 zeros bursts in three passes, 23 ladder rungs with one row of blocks in fp32 (22 with far B
lines), E146's `F6`, `D2` and `D4` again, and the K4 rungs in fp16 and int8, every check of C exact. The board at most 36.1 W, the
die 48–51 °C per burst; both stops tested on live samples first.

**Results** (`results.json`).
- **Past 32 MB the L3 keeps about half of a returning B panel:** 0.46–0.56 of the far B lines are L3-served from 39 to 59 MB in
  every family (0.997–1.012 below 32 MB).
- **Two one-row-block rungs at a power-of-two stride collapse:** `K6n4` (NB 4, KB 6) keeps 0.18 and `K4n8` (NB 8, KB 4) reads
  −0.20, 6.5% slower than if every far line came from DRAM; `F6`, `K6n4`'s layout with three row blocks, kept 0.33 (E146 0.327).
- **R2 NO SINGLE CURVE** as registered (rms 0.129 against 0.06); post hoc, off the two collapses one curve fits, C's lines
  occupying the L3 like the operands' (w 0.8–1.0, rms 0.026; C4).
- **R3 PASS ×3, the critic's rule as registered:** the fitted curve (ISO) predicts the time per op of `F6` (never fitted; E146 ran
  it on 2 November) to +0.41 / +0.26 / −0.76%, where E146's MAB read −10.0 / −10.3 / −9.4%; that pass rests on `K4n8`: refitted
  without it ISO misses `F6` by +8.0 / +7.9 / +5.9% (C1).
- **Out of sample on new shapes:** E146's ladder (18 cells with KB ≥ 2) 7.2% → 6.0% rms (R4 NOT BETTER by its 2-point bar), all 24
  cells 10.9% → 10.6%; `D2` and `D4` 13.1% → 6.3%.
- **Power:** zeros' burst power against E136's law P0 3.57 W rms with strict lines and 3.48 W with ISO's; P0 reads 2.7–4.4 W high
  on zeros.

**Scored:** R0 PASS; R1 INCONSISTENT (`K4n8`) and SOFT; R2 NO SINGLE CURVE; R3 PASS ×3; R4 NOT BETTER; R5 10 of 14; R6 every pass
reproduces; R7 P0 3.57 W rms.
**What it changes.** Item 1: where a large GEMM's L2-missing lines are served past the L3 is measured, and a new shape's time per
op comes to about 6% rms out of sample; what E146's MAB lacks is a block-stride term (the L3's set mapping, or the DRAM's channel
and bank mapping), to be registered with the pools' addresses logged. Open: the KB = 1 A lines, the vendor's binary, a second
card.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

## E159 — a large GEMM's sustained power and governed clock: G3 blind on three new shapes (Q139; card runs 2026-11-03, 05:33:58–05:52:55 PST, aifoundry2) — pre-registered

**Asked:** items 1 and 6 of section 11, and rank 3 of Q138's final critic: *"Register G3 (P0's power, closed through the frozen
chain and the trip rule) for 2-3 new GEMM shapes held 80-120 s in fp32 random, int8 full and zeros, with the predictions frozen
first. Rules: sustained clock within 15 MHz, board within 2 W, window die within 1 °C."* **Frozen first:** Q139(q)'s G3 with
E159's design and predictions (`91def7c4`, 04:16:13 PST; corrected in `ea38c8c3`, no frozen file changed). **Pre-registered** in
[`tools/claims-v3/e159/`](../../tools/claims-v3/e159/E159.md) (`d8badb73`, committed 05:31:46 PST, pushed by 05:32:04), before the
claim (05:32:57) and the first card touch (05:33:58). Data:
[`docs/reports/data/2026-11-03-e159-gemm-sustained/`](../reports/data/2026-11-03-e159-gemm-sustained/README.md) (`a84e8570`;
corrected after verification in `55a43b69`, C1–C7; no frozen file changed). aifoundry2's card 0 under its own governor, 65 W / 65
°C (low_power, 600 MHz, 519 mV) at the start, the end and after every run, nothing set; `dmcmd_audit` GO (19 READ sites); **18.95
card-minutes**; all nine holds ran (H1 `gemmL2x4x8`, H2 `gemmL4x2x4` and H3 `gemmL1x2x2`, each in fp32 random, int8 full and
zeros, twelve processes of 8 s), none cut, no stop fired; the board peaked at 76.1 W, the die mean at 66 °C; both stops tested on
live samples first.

**Results** (`results.json`).
- **Blind, G3 beats E136's law on all three:** the governed clock to **8.7 MHz rms** [5.2, 11.5] against 41.2, the board to **1.94
  W** [1.09, 2.60] against 3.99, the window die to **0.99 °C** [0.64, 1.26] against 1.35 (intervals over holds, post hoc); on the
  three fp32 random holds the law misses by 71.0 MHz and 6.48 W, G3 by 13.4 MHz and 2.16 W.
- **G3 FAILS every per-hold rule as registered:** clock 8 of 9, board 5 of 9, die 6 of 9 inside (the registered subset 5 of 6 on
  each).
- **What the misses are** (post hoc; C1, C2, C5): H1 fp32 random's clock FAIL, the only one, is the governor's 600–700 MHz dips
  (8.5% of its window), not P0's level; about 3 W (2.1–4.0) of its 7.2 W first-second deficit is its cold 46 °C start, a start-die
  term P0 lacks, every burst behind P0 having started at 47–56 °C; G3 reads zeros' board 2.4–2.5 W high on every shape, mostly the
  level (0.3–0.8 W given the first second).
- **Heat given the watts carries to a large GEMM, blind:** the chain on the measured trace reads all nine window dies within 1 °C,
  0.41 °C rms, so every die miss is the power path's.
- Like for like against Q139(q)'s 14 large GEMMs nested (12.8 MHz, 1.21 W, 0.69 °C), the blind board and die are worse and the
  clock better (C3).

**Scored:** G3 clock FAIL (8 of 9), board FAIL (5 of 9), die FAIL (6 of 9); G3's rms below E136's law's on all three; the chain on
the measured trace 9 of 9 inside 1 °C.
**What it changes.** Rank 3 is answered with a registered FAIL and a large gain: a governed large GEMM's sustained clock, board
and die follow from its burst levels, the frozen heat chain and the governor's trip to 8.7 MHz, 1.9 W and 1.0 °C rms, about five
times better than E136's law in clock. The next power terms, in order: a reference die for P0 at the leakage measured inside runs
(0.44–0.50 W/°C), the governor's clock in and between processes, and P0's per-shape level (rank 7) with a zeros level. Heat needs
no new term.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

## E160 — blind pool 6 under aifoundry2's own governor: M8's clock-and-voltage term in 6 s bursts, every launch's C checked (Q139; card runs 2026-11-03, 06:41:50–07:12:39 PST, aifoundry2; collected at 15:56 PST) — pre-registered

**Asked:** item 6 of section 11, and Q138's final critic's rank 2 on this card (*"aifoundry2's zeros level, a cap term rising with
overshoot and a D scale per format, E143"*), with the pool-6 half of rank 14's check of C. **Pre-registered** in
[`tools/claims-v3/e160/E160.md`](../../tools/claims-v3/e160/E160.md) (`2e016917`, committed 06:40:07 PST, pushed 06:40:09;
`d98d2e34` and Q139(k)'s `4946a992` its ancestors), before the claim (06:41:24–06:41:38) and the queue's start (06:41:50). The two
tiles whose 95% peak passed section 22's 82 W line, `fp32.anchor6_randn` and `fp32.real_qwen_softmax_v`, were dropped as
registered, with E80's fp32 random replicate, so 48 of the 50 were scored. **Collected late:** the session that ran it stopped at
about 07:46 PST on a usage limit, 34 minutes after the queue ended and before anything was collected; a resumed lane copied the
data read-only at 15:56 PST (D1, 8 h 44 min late), every one of the 221 code, tile and prediction files hashing on the box as
registered and its three binaries as pinned. Data:
[`docs/reports/data/2026-11-03-e160-blind6-aifoundry2/`](../reports/data/2026-11-03-e160-blind6-aifoundry2/README.md) (`6a330635`,
16:31:18; corrected after verification in `42ba850f`, C1–C7; no frozen file, measured number, prediction, rule or verdict
changed). aifoundry2's card 0 under its own governor, 65 W / 65 °C (low_power, 600 MHz, 519 mV) at the queue's start and end and
after every one of the 160 recorded runs and session reads, nothing set; **30.8 card-minutes**: the smoke, the board and die stop
tests (each tripped as designed, 64 and 58 ms after its line), then `s1`, 150 bursts in three passes (29.8 card-minutes). Every
command a READ; the board peaked at 79.37 W (no sampler line of 18,303 reached 80 W), the die mean at 62 °C; no POWER_SAFE or
thermal throttle in the service processor's ring.

**Results** (`results.json` › `score.rules`; measured − M8, rms over tiles, three passes each).
- **Every registered rule PASS:** F1, the burst clock on the 17 tiles M8 put below 795 MHz, **6.72 MHz**; F2 on the 31 pinned ones
  3.89 MHz; F3 48 of 48 inside ±16.9 MHz; W1, the governed board, **1.39 W** (better than its reference line); W2 1.73 W; W3 48 of
  48 inside ±3.50 W; H1, the die's rise, 0.67 °C; H3 45 of 48 inside ±1.41 °C; PK 47 of 48 tiles at or under M8's 95% peak; R0,
  the drift check.
- **Every launch's C bit-exact:** 2,596 of 2,596 in `s1` (fp32 824, fp16 706, int8 1,066), 2,620 with the smoke and the stop test;
  762 int8 launches carried a C word past the 32-bit wrap, all bit-exact: the first silicon reads of pool 6's new ranges.
- **Against the last blind score on this card** (M7 on pool 5, E143: governed 6.63 MHz and 1.60 W, pinned 1.01 MHz and 1.18 W,
  heat 0.585 °C; C2): the governed clock the same (6.72), the governed board a little better (1.39), the heat about the same
  (0.67); the pinned clock worse as registered (3.89; 0.47 MHz with the two mid-burst idle passes set aside, post hoc) and the
  pinned board worse (1.73 W, the intervals not overlapping). M7 reads pool 6's pinned tiles as M8 does (3.88 MHz, 1.72 W), so
  that comes from the pool or the session, not from M8.
- **In this session M8's one aifoundry2 change, the cap refitted from 65.58 to 65.20 W, read the governed tiles worse than M7 as
  frozen** (6.72 against 4.89 MHz, 1.39 against 1.10 W; an interval over tiles inside one session, C7): the capped tiles held
  66.72 W (66.89 W in E143), and the same model's mean governed clock error moved from −4.96 MHz in E143 to +2.82 MHz here.
- **fp16's burst D was 0.73 W high,** from its zeros anchor's idle first pass at 752 MHz (C1, post hoc); the three zeros anchors
  sit 1.49–1.85 W under M8 at 800 MHz.
- **E160-S, rank 5's aifoundry2 scale, NOT SCORED:** E153's D did not yet exist when E160 was reduced; reported against M8's
  predicted D600, floats 0.9737 and int8 0.8997.

**Scored:** F1, F2, F3, W1, W2, W3, H1, H3, PK, C and R0 PASS; S NOT SCORED.
**What it changes.** Item 6 on aifoundry2: M8 holds its bands under the governor at E143's size; in this session its cap refit was
no better than M7's cap. The post hoc candidates for the next terms there are a zeros level, a cap rising with overshoot and a
session term: the die started each burst 1.7 °C cooler on average than in E143, and the one tile run in both sessions ran 10.7 MHz
faster at the same watts, with the host binary changed too. Pool 6's real tiles are C-checked on this card.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it. Its claim was the last
text of the day's first post, which the stop left held and someone else later deleted (the dated note at the top of this file).

## E161 — heat over minutes predicted end to end from the bits under aifoundry2's own governor, with the sustained clock (Q139; card runs 2026-11-03, 16:25:00–17:30:05 PST, aifoundry2) — pre-registered

**Asked:** items 9, 8 and 6 of section 11, and rank 4 of Q138's final critic, its aifoundry2 half (*"about 1-1.3 °C governed on
aifoundry2"*). **Frozen first:** Q139(e)'s predictions (`455c46c1`) and A1 (`e224a3f8`). **Pre-registered** in
[`tools/claims-v3/e161/E161.md`](../../tools/claims-v3/e161/E161.md) (`ddbd15b1`, committed 16:22:46 PST and pushed 16:22:49,
written from about 15:55: C5), before the claim (16:24:03–16:24:16) and the go (16:24:34). Data:
[`docs/reports/data/2026-11-03-e161-heat-e2e-aifoundry2/`](../reports/data/2026-11-03-e161-heat-e2e-aifoundry2/README.md)
(`0158efd6`, time stamps `d1eb4fd6`; corrected after verification in `d0704105` and `77d8b89b`, C1–C5; the results, reduction and
post hoc reproduce byte for byte). aifoundry2's card 0 under its own governor, 65 W / 65 °C (low_power, 600 MHz, 519 mV) at the
session's start, at every step's start and end (14 reads) and at its end, nothing set; **35.07 card-minutes** of 40, about 65
minutes of wall time with E98's two 15-minute rests: G1Q (`int8.b_line_zero_ff` at 800 MHz) and G2Q
(`fp32.real_smol_softmax_v_causal`, in the trip regime), 13 minutes each. Every command a READ; the peaks 46.77 and 65.77 W, the
die means 63 and 66 °C; both stops tested on live samples first (die 62 ms, board 65 ms).

**Results** (`results.json`; from the bits and the idle card's die and board just before each hold, a whole-degree 47.0 °C anchor
known to ±0.5 °C: C2).
- **All six registered readings PASS:** G1Q's plateau 62.88 °C measured against 63.78 predicted (**−0.90 °C**, band 3.149), its
  trajectory 1.04 °C rms over seven checkpoints, its clock at or above 790 MHz for 0.952 of the last 300 s; G2Q's plateau 65.50 °C
  against the trip rule's 65.9 (**−0.40 °C**, band 0.60), in the trip regime as predicted (81% of the last 300 s under 790 MHz).
- **The sustained clock from the bits:** 680.1 MHz measured against 667.5 predicted (**+12.6 MHz**, band 35); with R at G1Q's
  measured 0.6875 °C/W (A1's C2) 656.0 MHz (+24.1), worse.
- **Given the watts,** Q138(t)'s chain reads both holds to 0.48 °C rms (plateaus +0.37 / −0.22 °C) on a fourth session; the power
  path's share, E − A, is −1.27 °C on G1Q and −0.17 °C on G2Q.
- **What the error is made of** (post hoc, corrected, C1): at the hold's kernel duty from its launch records, 0.970, G1Q's error
  goes from −0.90 to −0.39 °C and G2Q's clock error from +12.6 to +5.6 MHz (−0.88 to +0.12 °C and +12.3 to −2.2 MHz across six
  definitions of the share); M8's level inside the work, 0.91 W high on G1Q, is the larger part of its power-path error (with both
  corrected +0.44 °C, near arm A's +0.37); at the trip the terms do not separate.
- **Every launch's C bit-exact:** 3,354 of 3,354, G1Q's int8 operands reaching |128|.
- Between the holds the lane reduced G1Q and checked its C before G2Q began; G2Q ran as registered (C4).

**Scored:** all six registered readings PASS.
**What it changes.** Items 9, 8 and 6 on aifoundry2: heat over minutes from the bits reads about 1 °C at a plateau the bits set,
about 0.4 °C at the trip, and the sustained clock to about 13 MHz, from one session and two holds; rank 4's aifoundry2 half has
its first blind score, not its answer (C3). G1Q's band is 2.4–3.1 times the critic's 1.0–1.3 °C and does not separate M8 from M7
(−0.90 against −1.45 °C). Next: freeze the duty with its definition, end the scoring window at the hold's last process, carry M8's
level as a session or zeros-level term, then test on another session.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

## E162 — which operand pairing a GPU pays for, row against K, on A100 boards, and a later generation (Q139; GPU runs 2026-11-03, 04:32:51–05:05:14 PST, on Modal) — pre-registered; no lab card

**Asked:** item 2 of section 11, "A GPU measured the same way", and rank 9 of Q138's final critic: which operand pairing an A100
pays for, and a later generation with headroom under its cap, *"Rule: blind on held-out kinds on new boards, the row+K form beats
G1 by a resolved margin and is stable nested."* **Pre-registered** in [`tools/gpu/e162/E162.md`](../../tools/gpu/e162/E162.md)
(`038db597`, committed 04:32:27 PST, pushed 04:32:29) before any GPU call; amendments A1 and A2 (`0e8479c9`, 04:48:57: the L40S
arm stops by its rule; two stage-2 passes a board) before any stage-1 data was read; the freeze (`54e8f87d`, 04:54:02) before
stage 2. Data: [`docs/reports/data/2026-11-03-e162-gpu-row-k/`](../reports/data/2026-11-03-e162-gpu-row-k/README.md) (`c30af4f6`;
corrected after verification in `a78e754e`, C1–C8; no frozen file, rule, prediction or verdict changed). Reads only on the GPU.
**Metered $2.28**, under its $6 cap; the GPU checks together $12.67 of the owner's $20.

**Results.**
- **What an A100 pays for is A's rows one apart** (row-lag 1, A over B), not neighbours along K: a phase per row costs +38.5 to
  +48.0 W over a shared phase (W1); K neighbours are no pairing even with no row flips (W2 FAILS, −6.7 ± 2.8 W); consecutive rows
  are (W3, +60.4 ± 2.6 W); ROW > K in bf16, fp16 and int8 (W4, +58.9 / +44.6 / +21.3 W); the row wave costs 88.1 W on A against
  20.9 W on B (W6); R4b fails again.
- **Along K, pairs 8 or more apart cost where 1, 2 or 4 apart do not;** which lag from 8 up is not pinned: refitted at K-lag 16 or
  32 the same form predicts as well blind (15.8 and 16.2 W against 15.7, post hoc), so K-lag 8 is RK's stage-1 choice, not a
  finding (C1).
- **Blind, 22 never-measured kinds on two new boards:** the ridge row+K form RK reads **15.7 W rms [9.9, 21.8]** (10.1% of the
  154.7 W span) against E149's frozen G1 at 52.4 W [39.3, 64.1] (BEAT HOLDS, ratio 0.299) and E149's S1, the best form frozen
  before E162, at 36.1 W; fully nested, 14.9 / 15.5 / 13.1 W with the column scale recomputed in each fold (STABLE HOLDS).
- **The L40S** ran every window with its power-cap reason bit set, zeros included at 60–69% of its 350 W limit, so it holds no
  clock and its arm stopped by the registered rule (C4).
- HT1 HOLDS (1.28 against 3.33 °C), but it is BEAT in degrees through E149's linear heat model, not a heat test (C6).

**Scored:** V0, W1, W3, BEAT, BEAT_p, STABLE and HT1 HOLD; W4 and W5 ROW > K; W2 and R4b FAIL; the L40S's arm not scored.
**What it changes.** Item 2 is narrowed: the A100's pairing is A's rows one apart and, along K, pairs 8 or more apart; a toggle
model in that form predicts new kinds on new boards to 10% of the span, against 34% for the ET-SoC-1's pairing. Open: which K-lag
from 8 up, how zeros move along rows and along K (the two zero stripes are RK's worst kinds), a later generation under its cap, a
lower cap.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

E169's six frozen shapes (three at power-of-two strides, peaks at most 46.9 W) test *(dated, Wed 4 Nov 2026, after Q140's final critic, Q141(r): testing the index needs a step rule, which Q140 did not register; E169 reads the 8 MB index's step by a registered step rule, A2 (E169), `217c94a2` with its note 2 `5c9a62f1`)*.

M8's, M7's and M6's predictions (`75802201`, notes `3c9bb2ba`), M9 (`1f244140`, registered `8dfafae3`) and Q141(t)'s arm
(`67d5e44f`, A1 `c31e7ae3`), each an ancestor of the registration. **Pre-registered** in
[`tools/claims-v3/e163/E163.md`](../../tools/claims-v3/e163/E163.md) (`64dff136` 09:33:22 PST; the freeze `442474d5` 09:38:32; the
kit `1c47ce62` 09:52:17; amendment A1 `f13e0b39` 10:49:52), before the claim (14:11:27) and any launch. Data:
[`docs/reports/data/2026-11-04-e163-blind7-aifoundry3/`](../reports/data/2026-11-04-e163-blind7-aifoundry3/README.md) (`4293d151`, 16:54:31 PST, which also changed two lines
of E166's README in place; corrected after verification in a dated note, `3b102b85`, no verdict or number changed; and, in these
records, [dated note 2](../reports/data/2026-11-04-e163-blind7-aifoundry3/README.md.dated-note-2.md): its departure D1 was written down after the reduction, the route set in
the lane's script before the session) *(dated, Wed 4 Nov 2026, Q142's records: two more notes beside the README, [dated note 3](../reports/data/2026-11-04-e163-blind7-aifoundry3/README.md.dated-note-3.md), Q141(i)'s fix pass `1613f496` (dated note 2's drafts were four, not three; its conclusion stands), and [`README.md.dated-note-q142s.md`](../reports/data/2026-11-04-e163-blind7-aifoundry3/README.md.dated-note-q142s.md), Q142(s)'s `0d2cb5dd` (D1's collection route, the complaint, linked to its fix in runbook v3 and v3.1))*. aifoundry3's card 0 at 600 MHz in all 36,223 samples, TDP 0 W / 65 °C, max_power, 523 mV at
both ends, nothing set; **61.8 card-minutes** of 70, after E166 on the same card: the stop-path test 16302, the smoke 16301, three
kinds passes 16310–16312 (1,001–1,025 s) and E151's block 16350–16352, the budget rule stopping before 16313 as registered (FULL);
all three passes kept. Nine command kinds, all READs, two of them PMIC voltage-register reads (allowed); `since_reset_ms` −1 in
every sample; the board at most 54.3 W, `minshire[0]` 63 °C (stops 65 W / 70 °C).

**Results** (`scored.json` › `verdicts`: on Q139(u)'s footing, measured − M9).
- **Every launch's C bit-exact:** 4,062 of 4,062 (fp32 1,296, fp16 648, int8 2,118), KC PASS.
- **Power:** P1, fp32, **0.389 W**, FAIL (line [0.115, 0.283]), carried by the single high B bits; P2 fp16 0.192 and P3 int8
  0.191 W PASS; **B1**, the floats' bias, **+0.151 W, FAIL LOW** (line [−0.103, +0.042]); **B2**, int8's, **+0.051 W, FAIL LOW**;
  C1 42 of 48 inside M9's bands, PASS.
- **Against the older models:** **M1 (M9 − M6) −0.098 W [−0.148, −0.056], BETTER**; M2 (M9 − M8) −0.143 W and M3 (M9 − M7)
  −0.101 W, BETTER; U1 PASS. Over the 48, M9 / M8 / M7 / M6 read 0.278 / 0.420 / 0.379 / 0.376 W rms. **E163-RANK1 FAILS**, on B1
  and B2, not on M1 as forecast.
- **What carries the misses:** class S, the single high B bits, 0.577 W rms over seven tiles (five of C1's six outside); post hoc,
  without class S M9 reads 0.183 W over 41 kinds. Exponent-heavy fp32 0.181 W, whole-line int8 0.189 W and int8 with B held
  0.254 W, each resolved better than M6.
- **The session reads above M9:** the floats' anchors and random kinds +0.25 to +0.33 W over M9, and all 16 re-run tiles higher
  than in their earlier sessions (+0.013 to +0.278 W; K14 and K14' FAIL), while E151's block reproduced within 0.025 W (KE and KE'
  PASS). Post hoc this is a session-wide level or gain in D, not separated (the floats −0.100 ± 0.118 W + 1.09 ± 0.59% of D; the
  first dated note), never "a day-level term"; the card scale is reported only (1.0056 floats, 1.0112 int8), its PASS and FAIL
  labels readings of a reported item.
- **Heat:** H1–H3 0.226 / 0.163 / 0.124 °C rms, H4 46 of 48, PASS.
- **Real tiles:** R1 0.158 W rms over 10, R2 10 of 10 inside, PASS.
- **Q141(t)'s arm** (reported): over its 8 tiles 0.542 W rms with the three-point counts, 0.380 W with 27.

**Scored:** KC, P2, P3, C1, U1, H1–H4, R1, R2, KE and KE' PASS; M1, M2 and M3 BETTER; P1, B1, B2, K14 and K14' FAIL; E163-RANK1 FAIL.
**What it changes.** On M9's own card M9 is the most accurate model on new kinds, resolved, and int8's blind error falls from 0.296 W
(M8 on pool 6, E153) to 0.191 W; but the session sits above M9 in every format, which no registered term models, and the single high
bits stay the largest miss. Nothing frozen changes.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

## E164 — blind pool 7 on aifoundry1's card 1: M9 on the second 600 MHz card, the card term, rank 4's pair and rank 7's repeats, every launch's C checked (Q141; card runs 2026-11-04, 14:19:34–15:17:53 PST, aifoundry1's card 1) — pre-registered

**Asked:** item 6 of section 11, and rank 1 of the critic's list on the second 600 MHz card (E163 is aifoundry3's half), with rank
4 (a card's scale from a short run of random anchors, on the E163 and E164 pair), rank 7 (card 1's day-to-day repeat of eight pool-6
tiles against E157), rank 9 (the real tiles), the card term on pool 7 (X1, X2) and Q141(t)'s reported 27-point arm
(`card_half[1]`). **Frozen first:** as E163's. **Pre-registered** in
[`tools/claims-v3/e164/E164.md`](../../tools/claims-v3/e164/E164.md) (`4286f0a8` 09:18:57 PST, `f4e75c95` 09:41:51, `ce10e301`
10:05:44, amendment E164-A1 `45aeda12` 11:41:49), before the claim and any launch, and launched with `box/launch_e164_a1.sh`. Data:
[`docs/reports/data/2026-11-04-e164-blind7-card1/`](../reports/data/2026-11-04-e164-blind7-card1/README.md) (`5eecc457`, 17:28:54 PST, collected 17:04:25–17:14:34 after
E163, with rank 4's pair and Q141(t)'s arm; corrected after verification in a dated note, `708a0a04`: the S class's share as
registered, D1 written after the collection, K7W's widths; and, in these records, [dated note 2](../reports/data/2026-11-04-e164-blind7-card1/README.md.dated-note-2.md):
two post hoc readings, with their script). aifoundry1's card 1 (`V3_DEVICE=1`, its own lock) at 600 MHz in all 34,396 samples, 65 W
/ 65 °C, managed_power, 499 mV at both ends, nothing set; **58.3 card-minutes** of 65: the stop-path test 16402, the smoke 16401 and
four 96-run passes 16410–16413 (837–869 s), all kept, the budget rule stopping before 16414 as registered. E78's launch rule skipped
9 of the 384 runs (the die did not settle to its launch reading), so five tiles carry three passes; none was retried. Every command
a READ; `since_reset_ms` −1; the board at most 61.20 W, 63 °C (stops 66 W / 72 °C).

**Results** (`scored.json` › `verdicts`: on Q139(u)'s footing, card 1's slope; measured − M9 with M8's card term, 0.9616).
- **Every launch's C bit-exact:** 3,896 of 3,896 (PC, K7C and PCa PASS).
- **Power:** M9 0.376 / 0.175 / 0.200 W rms (P1 fp32 FAIL; P2 and P3 PASS), 0.273 W over the 48; **B1 +0.164 W, FAIL LOW**; **B2
  −0.076 W, FAIL HIGH**; **C1 41 of 48, FAIL**.
- **Against the older models:** **M1 (M9 − M6) −0.048 W [−0.111, +0.013], NOT RESOLVED**; M2 (M9 − M8) −0.118 W, BETTER; **U1
  +0.078 W, FAIL** (M9 resolved worse than M6 on the 12 kinds aimed at nothing). M8 / M7 / M6 read 0.390 / 0.332 / 0.321 W over the
  48. **E164-RANK1 FAILS**, so rank 1 fails on both 600 MHz cards.
- **Post hoc** ([dated note 2](../reports/data/2026-11-04-e164-blind7-card1/README.md.dated-note-2.md)): U1's FAIL is the card term. M6's and M7's frozen card-1
  predictions carry a float card term 1.0099 times M9's (0.9711 against 0.9616), while X1 reads the floats' measured ratio as M9's;
  scaling M9 by it takes the 8 float U tiles from 0.171 to 0.077 W rms (M6 0.081) and the floats' bias from +0.164 to +0.004 W. So
  U1 is B1 on the unaimed kinds, M6's 1% higher float card term absorbing the float excess both 600 MHz sessions show, never "M6
  models unaimed kinds better". Without class S M9 reads 0.191 W over 41.
- **The card term:** X1 PASS (the floats' switching 0.9621 of aifoundry3's, against M9's 0.9616); X2 FAIL (int8 0.9418).
- **Rank 4's pair, RK4:** PASS on both families: the random anchors' scale lands +0.07% (floats) and −0.54% (int8, a standard
  error of 0.51% against a forecast 1.35–1.64%) from the pool's median ratio.
- **Rank 7's repeat:** K7D FAIL (2 of 8 within 0.05 W of E157); K7W PASS, every tile inside its Welch half-width, 0.19–0.31 W on
  the floats and 0.22–0.33 W on int8, wider than planned on the floats only (the dated note); K7 FAIL, K7' PASS.
- **Heat:** H1, H2 and H4 PASS; H3 FAIL (int8 0.212 °C against [0.101, 0.185]); 0.334 / 0.352 / 0.212 °C rms, the die rising
  0.131 °C per board W against the 0.1151 carried.
- **Real tiles:** 0.171 W rms, 10 of 10 inside (R1, R2 PASS).
- **Q141(t)'s arm** (reported): the 27-point counts cut the S class's power rms by 41% here (0.543 → 0.318 W), 31% on aifoundry3
  and 36% pooled; R3, the share of its squared error they remove, is 0.66 / 0.53 / 0.59, never "53–66% of the rms".

**Scored:** PC, K7C, PCa, P2, P3, H1, H2, H4, R1, R2, X1, RK4, K7W and K7' PASS; M2 BETTER; P1, B1, B2, C1, U1, H3, X2, K7D and K7
FAIL; M1 NOT RESOLVED; E164-RANK1 FAIL.
**What it changes.** M9 reads new kinds on card 1 with the lowest rms of the four models, but not resolved from M6; the card term
should be read per family from the session's random anchors (the frozen 0.9616 holds for floats and is 2% high for int8, and the
anchors find both scales); the floats carry the same excess as on aifoundry3; card 1 heats faster per board W than M9 carries.
Nothing frozen changes.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

aifoundry2 part recomputed per tile from the session's own zeros anchors and die starts. Data:

in a dated note, `7830a9cb`: its check of C committed gzipped only, where the board's blind error sits, the dip range, the boot's
zone, which runbook ran). aifoundry2's card 0 under its own governor, 65 W / 65 °C at the queue's start and end, nothing set; its
checkout first fast-forwarded to `5e1bb309` (D1: `git pull --ff-only` refused over 905 local entries, 903 of them byte-identical to
the public main; they were tarred and stashed on the box, which still holds the tar, the stash and an old untracked copy of
`reduce_e77.py`). **38.4 card-minutes** of 40: `s1` ended at run 159 on `run_hpl.py`'s telemetry_stale guard, in a cool gate before
any launch, so 158 of 171 bursts counted (post hoc, consistent with a race in the guard's watcher; it failed safe). Every command a
READ; the residency query refused before the device opened; the board at most 81.16 W (stops above 85 W on three live samples, 76
°C and the 82 W admission).

**Results** (`results.json` › `score.rules`; 52 tiles, 12 governed and 40 pinned at the 800 MHz cap).
- **Every launch's C bit-exact:** 2,734 of 2,734.
- **Rank 2, E165-R2: FAIL, on resolution only.** G, the governed clock, **3.03 MHz rms** (mean −1.04), resolved better than M7
  ([−4.61, −1.95] MHz), PASS; Bg, the governed board, **0.71 W** (mean −0.43), and Bp, the pinned board, **0.99 W** (mean −0.02),
  each inside its numerical line, not resolved better than M7 (1.20 and 1.15 W; [−0.96, +0.04] and [−0.44, +0.08] W).
- F1 PASS; **F2 FAIL** (the pinned clock 5.11 MHz against 0.29–4.04, from six single-pass dips at 752–780 MHz); W1, W2, F3 (51 of
  52), H1 (0.455 °C) and H3 (52 of 52) PASS; **W3 FAIL** (45 of 52); C and R0 PASS.
- **Post hoc** (the dated note): the pinned clock's residual is a session term; the pinned board's largest errors are five class-S
  tiles at +1.80 to +2.52 W over M9, 57% of its squared error (0.695 W rms without them), and the six dips hold 22%; the governed
  board reads 0.43 W under M9 at the cap, with a 0.57 W spread.
- The session's zeros-anchor reads were written to `anchors-for-e167-e168.json` for E167's E2 arm and E168's anchors arm.

**Scored:** G, F1, W1, W2, F3, H1, H3, C and R0 PASS; Bg and Bp (not resolved), F2 and W3 FAIL; E165-R2 FAIL.
**What it changes.** M9's aifoundry2 part halves the blind governed-clock error (6.7 → 3.03 MHz; held out before, 3.68–5.12) and
reads the boards to 0.71 and 0.99 W (blind before, 1.4 W), but 12 and 40 tiles do not resolve the boards from M7's; the single high
bits carry most of the pinned board's error. Nothing frozen changes.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

## E166 — the heat replicate on aifoundry3: four 10-minute plateaus from the bits again, and a 25-minute large-rise hold (Q141; card runs 2026-11-04, 14:20:51–15:35:05 PST, aifoundry3) — pre-registered

**Asked:** items 9, 8 and 6 of section 11, and rank 3 of the critic's list ("heat over minutes from the bits, a second session per
card"), its aifoundry3 half (`card_half[3]`). **Frozen first:** Q140(e)'s predictions (`11174d20`, `c9aaf179`, `a4fe976d`,
`ac06c9be`). **Pre-registered** in [`tools/claims-v3/e166/E166.md`](../../tools/claims-v3/e166/E166.md) (`2b450e66` 09:16:36 PST, the
scorer `f830b9e2` 09:31:08, the kit `c75bc460` 09:41:09, dated note 2 `ae39563c` 11:24:33): R5 with G0, X1 and SL. Data:
[`docs/reports/data/2026-11-04-e166-heat-replicate-aifoundry3/`](../reports/data/2026-11-04-e166-heat-replicate-aifoundry3/README.md) (`179549ce`, 15:41:09 PST; two lines of its
README changed in place by E163's data commit `4293d151`, the figure right; corrected after verification in a dated note,
`3b102b85`, which names that change, the boot's zone and K_L's split). aifoundry3's card 0 at 600 MHz in all 44,488 samples, TDP 0
W / 65 °C at both ends, nothing set; **74.2 card-minutes** of 76: the smoke with its five stop tests, then the full design in one
pass, 16620, with no subset and no exclusion; G0 passed (the room 51.924 °C, at or under 53.0). Every command a READ, no
statistics reset; the board at most 44.2 W, 66 °C.

**Results** (`scored.json`; from the bits, M8's watts the registered primary; measured − predicted).
- **Every dispatch's C bit-exact:** 6,692 of 6,692.
- **RK3-a FAIL:** 2 of 4 holds inside the per-hold rule (K_Q and K_O's window). The four plateaus read **0.452 °C rms** from the
  bits (K_S −0.248, K_Q −0.014, K_L +0.869, K_O's window +0.037 °C), against E154's 0.370 on the same card; given this session's
  watts 0.281 °C; M7 0.289 and M6 0.217 °C through the same chain. K_L's +0.87 °C is about +0.47 °C of M8's watts and +0.40 °C of
  the heat path (the dated note).
- **RK3-c FAIL:** given the watts the large rises read 1.83 / 2.15 / 3.23% low (E154: 3.1–4.6%).
- **The long hold:** G_A reads SLOW STAGE SEEN (+0.158 °C, 0.008 °C over its line) while SL reads SLOPE NOT SEEN (−0.295 °C per 10
  minutes); K_OL's long window +0.371 °C. All five frozen bands PASS, and X1 found no exclusion.

**Scored:** G0, the five bands and X1 PASS; RK3-a and RK3-c FAIL; G_A SLOW STAGE SEEN; SL SLOPE NOT SEEN.
**What it changes.** Rank 3's aifoundry3 half fails on the replicate: from the bits the plateaus read worse than on E154, mostly
through M8's watts (M9 reads the whole-line class to 0.19 W against M8's 0.68 on pool 7, E163, but M8 was this run's primary), and
given the watts the chain reads every large rise low, with the sign E154 had: a gain deficit. *(Dated, Wed 4 Nov 2026, Q142's records, after Q142's round critic: a session level or a gain, not separated. Post hoc, E166's misses given the watts are flat in the rise and E154's grow with it: by least squares e = +0.395 °C − 1.43% of the rise in E166 (residual 0.062 °C) and −0.229 °C + 5.72% of the rise in E154 (0.076 °C); a level alone fits E166 better than a gain alone (0.079 against 0.155 °C), and a gain alone fits E154 better (0.110 against 0.204 °C); E144's one large rise was read to 0.1%. RK3-c registers the relative misses of large rises only, and no registered rule tests a level against a gain; M10 carries the chain's gain as a reported arm only (Q142's desk lanes, below). [Dated note 2](../reports/data/2026-11-04-e166-heat-replicate-aifoundry3/README.md.dated-note-2.md), with `posthoc_heat_level_gain.py` and its output.)* The slow stage is undecided, its two
readings disagreeing. Nothing frozen changes.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

0 under its own governor after E167, 65 W / 65 °C read at the queue's ends and after every run, nothing set; E136's `rgm136`
binaries; **47.3 card-minutes** (the session cap 58, at most 63 in all), all 10 holds complete, none cut or refused by the 82 W
admission. Every cold gate timed out after 360 s at 48–49 °C, so those holds started there, as registered. Every command a READ; the
board at most 76.59 W.

**Results** (`results.json`, `reduce.txt`; measured − G4).
- **A7, G4 on at least 8 of 9 holds: the clock FAILS** (6 of 9 within 15 MHz; 30.2 MHz rms, mean −21.3): S3's shape runs −58.9 and
  −62.5 MHz under G4, and under every model; post hoc the other seven read 10.8 MHz rms. **The board PASSES** (9 of 9, 0.82 W rms,
  mean +0.18) and **the window die PASSES** (9 of 9, 0.51 °C, mean +0.46). Both int8 increments PASS (+0.10 and +0.80 W against
  ±1.328). The registered subset, the six fp32 random holds: the clock FAILS (4 of 6), board and die PASS. R0 PASS (2,425 kernel
  records, every one ok).
- **A2 (reported): OPPOSITE SIGN.** The cold minus warm int8 zeros window board reads −0.777 W (starts 49 and 56 °C) against G4's
  +1.079 W (h 0.54 W): the board falls with the start, the direction of a leakage law in the absolute die, so G4's start-referenced
  law has the wrong sign; unambiguous at the day's starts (the kit's note had checked rest floors of 46–48 °C; the dated note).
- Before: G3 blind on E159, 8.7 MHz / 1.94 W / 0.99 °C; G4 held out, 10.3–11.1 MHz / 1.33–1.47 W / 0.58–0.63 °C. On these holds G3
  as frozen reads 30.9 MHz / 0.74 W / 0.38 °C and E136's law 113.5 / 7.97 / 3.06.

**Scored:** the board, the die, both increments and R0 PASS; the clock FAILS, so A7 FAILS; the subset's clock FAIL; A2 OPPOSITE SIGN.
**What it changes.** G4 holds a governed large GEMM's board and die blind on new shapes (0.82 W, 0.51 °C), as G3 as frozen does on
these holds, but not its clock at one shape's geometry, and its start dependence has the wrong sign. Nothing frozen changes.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

## E169 — the stride shapes: the L3's 8 MB index read by a registered step rule (Q141; card runs 2026-11-04, 17:06:32–17:17:49 PST, aifoundry2) — pre-registered

**Asked:** item 1 of section 11, rank 6 of the critic's list, and Q140's final critic's fault 3, the step rule (`card_half[6]`).
**Frozen first:** Q140(q)'s B6 and B7 and their predictions (`49575094`, `e39d06da`, `1bfaa4e4`). **Pre-registered:** amendment A2
(E169) with the step rule, [`tools/claims-v3/e169/REGISTRATION-A2.md`](../../tools/claims-v3/e169/REGISTRATION-A2.md) (`217c94a2`
09:22:23 PST); the scorer `18aaca80` (09:33:28); the kit `6a3929ec` (09:46:17); its notes `2bf8f092` (09:47:26) and `5c9a62f1`
(10:44:17). Data: [`docs/reports/data/2026-11-04-e169-stride-shapes/`](../reports/data/2026-11-04-e169-stride-shapes/README.md) (`d878ada6`, 17:29:34 PST; its verifier
asked for no dated note). aifoundry2's card 0 under its own governor, last after E165, E167 and E168, 65 W / 65 °C read before,
after and after every run, nothing set; E136's binary with every shape an argument; **11.3 card-minutes** of 20, 50 bursts, none
refused. Every command a READ; the board at most 36.95 W. Its launch and collection ran on the runbook's v2.

**Results** (`results.json` › `scores`: five passes of fp32 zeros).
- **The registered verdict on the 8 MB index: A STRIDE STEP SM DOES NOT SIZE.** Both power-of-two panel strides step: P1/N1 +30.00%
  [+29.36, +30.65] against the L3 set map's frozen +37.06% (0.81 of it), and P3/N3 +27.11% [+26.53, +27.69] against +30.97% (0.88);
  ISO and STRICT predict no step. P2/N2 (reported) +4.47%, which supports ISO.
- B7 FAILS, as registered to fail (SM's level 13.3–22.0% above the measured time on all six shapes); the ties to E158 read TIED; R0
  PASS (50 runs exact).
- Each model's level over the six shapes: ISO 10.2%, STRICT 15.4%, SM 18.7% rms (E158: ISO 6–11%, with misses of 8–13% at
  power-of-two strides).

**Scored:** A STRIDE STEP SM DOES NOT SIZE; B7 FAIL, as expected; TIED; R0 PASS.
**What it changes.** A power-of-two panel stride costs time per op, about 27–30% here, which ISO's level misses; the L3's set map
gets the sign and 0.81–0.88 of the size. A stride term's size needs its own registration before new shapes test it. Nothing frozen
changes.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

## E170 — blind pool 7 on aifoundry1's card 0 under its own governor, 6 s bursts at the 800 MHz cap: a third card, which no model had seen (Q141; card runs 2026-11-04, 16:19:41–16:48:53 PST, aifoundry1's card 0) — pre-registered

**Asked:** item 6 of section 11: rank 1 of the critic's list on a third card, and a card nobody has measured (the Q139 critic's rank
4; the Q138 critic's rank 5). Card 0 had been kept out of use since E84 (31 October), whose PMIC over-power alarm tripped on fp32
random data; the owner put it back in use with his go, at about 13:56 PST: *"There was a problem with card 0, but that's been
fixed."* What the fix was is not known. **Pre-registered** by Q141(w) in [`tools/claims-v3/e170/E170.md`](../../tools/claims-v3/e170/E170.md)
(`b5ca3e78` 14:41:25 PST: the registration, its card term set blind and its predictions; the scorer `574dc5b5` 14:48:30; the kit
`57e1c6c4` 15:13:43; amendment E170-A2 `6147a9c7` 16:08:53; the kit's corrections `0e35ec58` 16:09:38), every one before any card-0
data. *The last two had no independent verifier read before the launch, and `0e35ec58` edited four kit files in place (its README,
`box/launch_e170.sh`, `harness-e170.json` and `selftest_kit_e170.py`), adding refusals only; every registration file, scorer,
protocol and prediction is as registered, and the box's launch hashes equal git at `efaaa726` (the data README's dated note).* Data:
[`docs/reports/data/2026-11-04-e170-blind7-card0/`](../reports/data/2026-11-04-e170-blind7-card0/README.md) (`81178664`, 17:36:18 PST, collected 17:18:17–17:21:42;
corrected after verification in a dated note, `708a0a04`: its provenance, E170-W's line, the day's post). Card 0 (`V3_DEVICE=0`, its
own lock) under its own governor: 65 W / 65 °C, low_power, the clock at its 600 MHz idle, 519 mV at both ends, nothing set. **Its
state was read first, on READ paths: GO** (module uptime 13,098 min against E84's 7,240 + 5,857.7: no service-processor reset since
E84, its identity by uptime continuity, never the firmware-revisions query). **29.2 card-minutes** of 40, after E164 had ended on card
1 (never two of our sessions on aifoundry1 at once): the die and board stop-path tests, a ramped smoke (a low-power tile, then one
short burst of `fp32.randn_a24k`, fp32 random with A 2:4 sparse, a card-0 estimate of 58 W, as registered, because E84 read
full-density fp32 random at 72.97 W on card 0), then the full plan, 135 of 135 bursts. Every command a READ; the residency query
refused by the guard before its reader started; the highest sample **59.38 W**, 6.52 W under the 65.9 W stop and about 15.6 W under
the 75 W alarm; the service processor's ring held no PMIC over-power line and no POWER_SAFE record in 1,704 new entries.

**Results** (`results.json`, `score.txt`; the burst board, measured − M9 through a card term set blind: the session's own zeros
level in full and κ0 = 0.9463).
- **Every launch's C bit-exact:** 2,373 of 2,373.
- **E170-W PASS:** **0.727 W rms** over 39 tiles against the registered rule's line, [0.599, 1.040] W (20,000 draws; E170.md's
  printed 0.602–1.045 W is its 4,000-draw planning figure); **E170-B PASS** (mean −0.090 W); M8 0.844, M7 0.804, M6 0.816 W.
- **M1 BETTER** than M6 on the burst D (−0.158 W [−0.242, −0.094]) and **M2 BETTER** than M8 (−0.246 W); M9's burst D 0.791 W
  (M6 0.949, M8 1.037).
- **X0 FAIL:** the switching scale reads 0.977 [0.963, 0.987] against κ0's 0.9463 (post hoc, referred to each tile's die start,
  0.931).
- F PASS (the clock 3.98 MHz rms); W3 PASS (34 of 39); **H1 and H3 FAIL** (the burst rise 2.46 °C rms, mean +2.38: the die rose
  about 0.24 °C per board W against the burst law's 0.14); C and S PASS.
- Post hoc: without class S, 0.511 W rms over 31 tiles; class S reads +0.72 W on average.

**Scored:** W, B, F, W3, C and S PASS; M1 and M2 BETTER; X0, H1 and H3 FAIL.
**What it changes.** On a card no model had seen, M9's governed relation with a card term from three zeros bursts per format and one
constant predicts the burst board to 0.73 W rms, and M9 is resolved better than M6 there; the burst heat does not carry, so a
coefficient per card is needed, and κ0's footing needs the die-start referral. E170 was designed never to approach card 0's 75 W
alarm, so it says nothing about the fix. Nothing frozen changes.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

## E171 — blind pool 8 on aifoundry3 at 600 MHz pinned: M10 scored blind beside M9 on 31 new kinds, 12 re-runs and the anchors (Q142; card runs 2026-11-05, 11:38:12–12:35:33 PST, aifoundry3) — pre-registered

**Asked:** item 6 of section 11, with items 8 and 9 through the 4.5 s heat: M10, the model's registered refit, scored blind on a pool
drawn by an independent designer who never saw a model's errors (Q142's round critic's `next` rank 1). **Pre-registered** by Q142(k6) in
[`tools/claims-v3/e171/E171.md`](../../tools/claims-v3/e171/E171.md) (`4441083f` to `86d10a75`, with its dated notes 1 and 2, the 600 MHz
scorer and its v2 and v3, the kit and runbook through `1a76a870` and the v3 drivers `e2b48f5a`/`6f4b48e4`), on pool 8's predictions
frozen in `4576fb90` (Thu 5 Nov 02:30:56 PST), every one before any claim. Run on the owner's go of Thu 5 Nov 2026 about 11:15 PST,
*"feel free to use all 4 cards for experiments, no need to wait, [removed: a sentence about machine access]"*. Data:
[`docs/reports/data/2026-11-05-e171-blind8-aifoundry3/`](../reports/data/2026-11-05-e171-blind8-aifoundry3/README.md) (`91f8ae40`, 13:10:18 PST;
corrected after verification in dated note 1, `d34f2be6`, 15:08:19; P8-KC's own count in dated note 2, these records). aifoundry3 card 0,
600 MHz pinned (TDP 0 W / 65 °C / max_power / 600 MHz / 523 mV at both ends, nothing set): **57.35 card-minutes** of 70, the FULL plan, four
kinds passes kept (17110–17113), the smoke and the stop-path test PASS, E78's launch rule skipping one run; 600 MHz in all 33,818 samples;
peaks 54.77 W and 63 °C against stops of 65 W and 70 °C.

**Results** (blind: measured − predicted against pool 8's frozen predictions, W rms with the bias; N1 per format).
- **M10 0.180 W rms (+0.070) over 31 new kinds:** fp32 0.176 (+0.112, n 11), fp16 0.238 (+0.065, n 8), int8 0.131 (+0.035, n 12); M9
  0.245 W (+0.122). Held out on this card, for comparison: fp32 0.202, fp16 0.148, int8 0.170 W.
- Class S, the 8 single-high-bit tiles: M10 0.273 W (+0.195), M9 0.402 W (+0.298).
- The 4.5 s heat through M10's law: 0.187 / 0.193 / 0.123 °C by format (M9's law 0.197 / 0.207 / 0.114 °C).
- The session's float scale s 1.00615, the median of six anchors.
- Every launch's C bit-exact: the check of C 3,490 of 3,490; P8-KC's own count 2,550 of 2,550 (dated note 2).

**Scored:** P1 PASS 0.176; **P2 FAIL** 0.2381 against its 0.2374 line; P3 PASS 0.131; **B1 FAIL LOW** +0.092 [−0.097, +0.064]; B2 PASS;
C1 PASS 30 of 31; **M1 BETTER** −0.065 W [−0.122, −0.012], M1f BETTER, M1i NOT RESOLVED; **A1 BETTER** −0.062 W; **KA PASS**; H1–H4 PASS;
LG NOT SEPARATED; **P8-G FAIL** (on P2 and B1); HM NOT SCORED. *Quote it with dated note 1:* M1 BETTER, A1 BETTER and KA PASS hold on the
registered footing (Q139(u)'s, frozen beforehand); with the leakage fitted on the session and the dips removed (s 0.996), M1, M1f and A1 are
NOT RESOLVED and KA FAILS; M1 is negative in all eight footings and resolved in five; P8-G FAILS in every footing. It is **the first blind
score of M10 at 600 MHz in its own form** (E173 had scored M10 carried by rule E first, at 12:47:48).
**What it changes.** M10's held-out power figure became blind on aifoundry3 and held overall (0.180 W against M9's 0.245). Post hoc (the
card day's critic, never a verdict): nearly all of the floats' excess after the session scale is class S, +0.018 W without the 8
single-high-bit tiles. Departure D1, wording only: the day's post said "for 2 h" where E171.md says 1.5 h. Verified: holds with fixes (1
major, 4 minor, all wording), answered by dated note 1.
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

[`docs/reports/data/2026-11-05-e176-m11-holds-aifoundry3/`](../reports/data/2026-11-05-e176-m11-holds-aifoundry3/README.md) (these records),
headed "G0-REFUSED: no hold ran, nothing scored, not a failed prediction", under

600 MHz / 523 mV at both ends, nothing set); 600 MHz in 3,776 of 3,776 samples; peaks 51.55 W and 57 °C (firmware), in the smoke.

**The readings** (measured; the rows marked post hoc decide nothing). The card's idle floor at the smoke's first sample (17:21:17, 4 h 46
min after E171 ended): 53.202 °C 34-sensor mean, firmware 52 °C, board 23.97 W, already above G0's line. R0 fell from 54.81 to 53.49 °C
after the smoke (−1.69 °C per 10 min over its last 240 s), so G0's window sat about 0.38 °C above the floor. *(dated, Thu 5 Nov 2026, after Q143(r)'s verifier: 54.81 °C is the measured pass's first sample, 3.3 s before R0 began, and 53.49 °C rounds R0's sample at +299 s; R0 fell from 54.75 °C at its start to 53.48 °C at its end. 2–3 °C between sessions and within a day (E154 50.98 °C, E166 51.84 °C, firmware 49 °C at E171 this morning, 53.20 °C this evening). **No
later try:** the room rule the lane wrote first (17:34:16 PST, before its comparison window) needed the host's NVMe composite at least
0.5 °C under its baseline, and it read 38.850 °C in every sample of five windows; *post hoc*, that sensor reports whole kelvins, so the rule
could only show a cooling of 1 °C or more.

it, a registration answers G0's fixed line against a floor that moves. Two defects the session showed (the q143k runbook's
[dated note 2](../../tools/claims-v3/q143k-runbook/README.md.dated-note-2.md)): the rest rule of step 11 counted the dry session's "session
end" (40 minutes lost; the outcome unaffected), and the scorer writes before the gates (the code verifier's fault 2, seen on real data);

evening's critic re-derived it (G0's room 53.5819 °C from 1,200 of 1,200 samples by its own code). *(dated, Fri 6 Nov 2026, Q148(r)'s records: E181's kit lane found an E174-class defect inherited from this kit. `run_e176.py` calls E97's `check_reused()`, which reads eight files, and one of them, `tools/claims-v3/e89/reduce_e89.py`, is in neither E176's box list (`frozen-e176.sha256`) nor the q143k runbook's step 7 sync; the session ran only because aifoundry3 kept a matching copy. Nothing E176 read or recorded changes; E181's box list names the file ([E176's kit note 4](../../tools/claims-v3/e176/README.md.dated-note-4.md)).)*
**Report:** no page of its own; the data README is the public record, and the kinds page (A25) carries it.

## E177 — M11's holds on aifoundry2 (Q143; reserved, optional) — not built, not run

Reserved by Q143 as M11's optional aifoundry2 session, from E167's kit. **Not built, not run:** no kit, no aifoundry2 reader in M11's scorer
(both versions refuse `--a2`), no runbook, no verifier or readiness check; the readiness critic read it NOT READY, and no `e177` path exists
in any ref. Never "failed". M11's registration recommends no aifoundry2 card time (HM-LG-A2's power 29% with two sessions, 51% with four, at
about 58 card-minutes each).

**Q142(h2) and Q142(a2), beside the register** (Thu 5 Nov 2026, about 09:35–11:55 PST; desk only: no card, no device command, no lab box,
no post; new files only in `tools/claims-v3/m11/`; each lane read by a separate verifier and corrected in new files, then a round critic).
**Q142(h2)**, card 1's heat-scale and int8 readers and M11's refit (the pool-8 readiness critic's `next` ranks 2 and 7): the v1 reader
`read_hc1b_i8pb.py` (`21666b23`, 10:07:38 PST; self-test 15 of 15; never run by the card day) and the refit on the committed rows for (c)
HC1 and (d) I8P (`2b33103c`, 10:15:36): **neither enters**, decided by the registered rules on committed rows (held out): HC1 fails only
its two-thirds clause (9 of 14 sessions, 10 needed), I8P its pooled clause (+0.00036 W, the interval across 0) and E164's "falls and keeps its
sign" (−0.081 → +0.058 W). Its verifier (about 10:20–10:45) held with fixes: one major fault (v1 refused, exit 6, any session that set a pass
aside, as 14 of the 45 committed reductions did), one minor, two nits and one carried; the fix pass froze the v2 reader
(`read_hc1b_i8pb_v2.py`, `ea12f108`, 11:13:51: self-test 24 of 24, `SHA256SUMS-h2-reader-v2` 129), before the owner's go and the card day's
first launch, so HC1-B's and I8P-B's code is frozen before the data. **Q142(a2)**, M11's amendment A2 (`d3d97fe2`, 10:07:42): an in-session
calibration reading, HM-CAL, the H_E and Z line read on L1, S and L2; its verifier (about 10:15–10:40) held with fixes (F1, minor: A2 left
out section 0.1's E144 reading; a nit, wording and precision, the result's nits); the fix pass wrote A3 (`ed24bc6e`, 11:07:48): if A2's
premise holds, on E154 then E166, HM-CAL reads BETTER in 99.2% and enters in 97.1%, a blind 0.104 °C [0.058, 0.166] against 0.278 °C
unscaled; in section 0.1's case (H_E read by the chain) BETTER in 37.1% and enters in 21.6%. Both are registered, design only. **The round's
critic** (about 11:18–11:55) found a design gap (A3 quantified the case that breaks A2's premise but registered no reading that survives

records), a coverage nit (it stood in for the round's unread fix passes) and stale next-step lists in Q142(r3)'s records (answered by

the card day): E175's registration, draw, frozen predictions and kit (`c50ff493` to `16b3d6e3`, above); its verifier held with fixes (five
minor items: A2-M1's power and its reading, two process slips, two nits and a carried item); the fix pass wrote dated note A2 (`73125272`, 14:57:16: on the 24 kept
tiles M9's part sits a near-constant +0.034 W above M10 carried, so A2-M1 reads the session's board level; a desk reader after the

## E178 — one-number matrices on the TensorFMA: 40 kinds, each tile filled with one number, the flips forecast first (Q146; registered, amended by A1 and A2, kitted with kit note 1; aifoundry3 card 0 at 600 MHz pinned) — not run

**Run on 6 November, blind** *(dated, Fri 6 Nov 2026, Q149's records; the heading above is as first written)*: FULL on aifoundry3 card 0 at 600 MHz pinned, the card day Q149, after E181's step 22; the session 11:33:24–12:18:36 PST, **45.2 card-minutes** of 60; all four passes kept, 299 of 304 runs (five skipped by E78's approach rule, each approach ending at 59 °C over the 58 °C launch line, never retried); peaks 54.06 W and 62 °C; nothing set; every device command a read. The owner's sentence: not measured. **Quoted as its dated note 1 corrects the README** ([note 1](../reports/data/2026-11-06-e178-one-number-aifoundry3/README.md.dated-note-1.md), `f68f0380`, 13:57:23, after the data verifier's 3 minor faults): (1) equal flips read equal by E178-E in all three formats, at the rule's registered resolution (no rule may claim equality finer than 0.2 W); inside the groups three reported pairs resolve: int8 4 − 8 −0.067 W [−0.124, −0.010], fp16 2 − (−1) −0.175 W [−0.250, −0.100] and fp16 4 − (−1) −0.146 W [−0.166, −0.125]; (2) his pair: +0.053 W at the board, positive in each of the four passes, its four-pass 95% interval [+0.022, +0.085] above 0, never "resolved in all four passes"; post hoc, the four pass means agreed more closely than their couples predict (0.020 W against about 0.077 W; 2.5% of draws), while the couples scattered as registered (0.126 W against 0.125 W); (3) the public-text scan read 133 text files, the text in 16 `.gz` files and 129 of the 196 tiles; (4) the post's "until ~13:49 PST" counted 1.5 h from E178's handover (12:19:16); (5) the score preceded step 22's last three gates, as the kit registers. *(dated, Fri 6 Nov 2026, after Q149's records' verifier: line (2) above is abridged; note 1's line 2 reads, verbatim, “His pair: +0.053 W at the board, positive in each of the four passes, its four-pass 95% interval [+0.022, +0.085] above 0 (E178-H-int8 POSITIVE). The data directory holds 359 files.

**Asked (Q146):** a matrix filled with one number against a matrix filled with another, with the transistor flips forecast first, then
measured (02's row). **What it is:** two 16×16 tiles, each filled with one number, multiplied and added into a running sum C by the
TensorFMA, about 540,000 (fp32) or 910,000 (int8) times in a half-second launch. No input bit ever changes, so what flips is the running
sum, walking 0, p, 2p, 3p…, and two buses that drop to zero between uses. **The 40 kinds, chosen by a stated rule:** int8 1–8, 85, 127, −1
and A = 4, B = 5 both ways; fp32 and fp16 0.5–8, −1 and 4, 5 both ways; and fp32 1.5 × 2⁻⁶⁴, whose products underflow, so its sum never
moves. **Registered** `48171930` (20:51:01 PST Thu 5 Nov; 61 files, `SHA256SUMS-e178-registration` 76). **Amended** by
[A1](../../tools/claims-v3/e178/E178.md.dated-note-A1.md) `f421c67a` (22:12:30; 8 files, `SHA256SUMS-e178-a1` 27): E178-S-int8 registered
(int8 85s against neg_ones, +0.355 W from the flips, negative under any held-1-bit model), and the one-number vector add's "null"
withdrawn: the float write-back returns to zero between results, so a one-number float add pays a popcount-of-the-result term (`fadd.pi`
open); and by [A2](../../tools/claims-v3/e178/E178.md.dated-note-A2.md) `c212a345` (00:05:00 Fri 6 Nov, committed alone; 2 files,
`SHA256SUMS-e178-a2` 17): the SIZE words in the scorer's terms (A1-1's "FAIL LOW" is the case the scorer prints as FAIL HIGH: the forecast
above the measured interval), the read limit with D(85) − D(127) a reported contrast, the Checked lines, and the per-electron forecast
named october-forecast-model, with its freeze line's form. **Kitted** [`a4aafc6f`](../../tools/claims-v3/e178/KIT.md) (23:06:28 Thu; 47
files, `SHA256SUMS-e178-kit` 72), with [kit note 1](../../tools/claims-v3/e178/KIT.md.dated-note-1.md) `763307e7` (01:56:48 Fri; 21 files,
`SHA256SUMS-e178-kit-note1` 47): the kit verifier's faults fixed before any datum (sync v2 from the harness, s03 v2, s22 v2, collect v2,
`scrub_tree_e178.py`); box steps 2, 5 and 11 run their v2 files. *(dated, Fri 6 Nov 2026, after Q148(r)'s verifier: kit note 1's four fixes, each by its section: (1) the sync takes every file the harness names but the box binaries, closing the E174 class; (2) E178-R-int8 is read on the scored P, as registered, with its power at that reading; (3) the login check reads every directory of the output, and run_e78's own stop line; (4) a failed arm is dropped, never left in the output, a collection rule registered before any datum.)* **Not run; 0 card-minutes.** Planned: four passes of all 40 kinds, about
45–54 card-minutes (cap 60), on the same card after E181. *(A dated note A3, `77768027`, 05:17:12 PST Fri, a reported minion-rail arm, was
committed by the next desk round beside these records; its verifier had not reported at these records.)*

**The frozen forecasts** (registered, not measured: M10's prices on the open-RTL bench's counts, above zeros, aifoundry3):

| Pair | Forecast flips per op | Forecast power difference |
|---|---|---|
| int8 5s against 4s | 23,360 against 10,240 | **+0.186 W** (P; F +0.188 W); power 0.90 at 4 kept passes, 0.64 at 3, 0.23 at 2 |
| fp32 5s against 4s | 28,291 against 23,888 | +0.010 W, below resolution |
| fp16 5s against 4s | 28,364 against 23,997 | +0.017 W, below resolution |
| int8 85s against neg_ones (E178-S-int8) | — | +0.355 W (power 0.93) |
| int8 85s against 1s | 42,654 against 10,240 | +0.465 W |
| fp32 (A 5, B 4) against 1.5 × 2⁻⁶⁴ | — | +0.092 W |
| fp16 6 against 0.5 | — | +0.021 W |
| U: fp32 1.0 against 1.5 × 2⁻⁶⁴ (the sum moving or not) | — | +0.075 W (P), +0.131 W (F); power 0.31 / 0.67 |

int8 1, 2, 4 and 8 are forecast identical; the slope is about 7.5 fJ a flip (7.484 fJ); the floats' 8.2 W above zeros is live lanes and
clocked registers, the same for every nonzero number, so the floats' 5s-against-4s gaps (+0.010 to +0.038 W across the arms) are below the
card's resolution. **What it cannot tell** (Q146's critic, fault 2): no E178 rule separates the accumulator's flips from a cost per 1-bit it
holds at each read (a precharged read the open RTL does not have; the silicon's register file is full custom); only 85 against 127 would,
and that pair is reported only (A2). Post hoc (A1-3), M10 reads the committed int8 one-number rows 0.13–0.22 W high, and 96% of its
5s − 4s is the bus term: if the card pays half M10's price, 5s − 4s resolves only 45% of the time (87% with a second session).
**Corrections, quoted as corrected:** the scout's "int8 TenC flips not counted (c_regfile 0)" is wrong: `settle_count` files
`.lane_tima.tenc_rf` under `lane_operand_bus`, so M10's int8 bus term carries the accumulator; M10's `c_end` is value-blind for one-number
fills (7,936 for 4s, 5s, pi and the underflow kind alike) and counts flips for denormal products the silicon flushes; the kit's Intel dry
pass is **18 of 18** checks, not "19 of 19"; KIT.md line 115's 7.4796 fJ is 7.484 fJ a flip; P06 is checked, not used; v1 s07's header
omits E78's six anchor tiles; E178.md section 5's R-int8 power line reads "0.994–0.998 if P's order is right; 0.025 if every kind costs the
same" at the scorer's reading; the verifier's n0+1 figures are A1-6's −0.0059 to +0.0044 W a kind and 0.0000 W on 5s − 4s; "power_e178.py
line 77" is line 89; the launch sizes are single runs, not medians (A1-6's medians 539,554.5 / 539,802 / 913,527.5); the bench README's job-2
time is 20:25:55–20:27:14; section 4's "within 1%" holds pair by pair (the groups span 4.17% / 4.34%); the kit verifier's fault 3: on
aifoundry3 run_e78's stop entry is a uid, not a login, and E164's `--check` misses that line in any directory; its fault 4: the desk paths
come from a failed arm's reduce log, not the scorer's repository-relative error string; A2's header says "to about 00:30 PST", written
before its commit at 00:05:00 (its re-runs ended 00:14:03). The two committed desk-test logs whose counts depend on where they ran (the v2
runbook test's file count, the v2 collection test's path hits) are quoted by their verdict lines, 31 of 31 and 6 of 6.
**Checked before any datum** (the critics' re-runs, desk only): every `--check` passes (kinds 40, forecast, plan, flips A1, kit, harness

## E179 — the public power viruses on a 600 MHz card (Q147, the owner's item 4) — number assigned; not registered, not built, not run

Assigned by Q147 to his go for the public power viruses on a card. **Not registered, not built, not run:** no registration, forecast,
rule, kit or scorer exists, and no `e179` path is in any ref. Never "failed". The number stays assigned, and whether and how it goes on is
the owner's decision. E180 covers one of its two purposes: a measured fixed-operand reference for the vector unit.

## E180 — one-number vector adds: a matrix of 4s against a matrix of 5s, added on the vector unit (Q146(f); registered, amended by A1, kitted with kit v2; aifoundry3 card 0 at 600 MHz pinned) — not run

**Run on 6 November, blind** *(dated, Fri 6 Nov 2026, Q149's records; the heading above is as first written)*: FULL on aifoundry3 card 0, after E178's step 22; the session 12:54:50–13:36:52 PST, **42.0 card-minutes** of 50; all six passes kept, 47 of 47 bursts each; box step 6's first compile on a box read BUILD OK; peaks 44.70 W and 57 °C; nothing set; every device command a read. Its dated notes [M1](../../tools/claims-v3/e180/E180.md.dated-note-M1.md) (`b38d09e3`, 09:49:37 PST) and [M2](../../tools/claims-v3/e180/E180.md.dated-note-M2.md) (`2b32db9d`, 10:42:13, after M1's verifier's 5 minor faults, with the reader `arm_m1_e180.py`, self-test 15 of 15), both before any datum, give october-forecast-model's arm as reported: 5s − 4s +9.09 fJ an instruction, +0.0042 W at E180's registered rate. **The verdicts** ([data README](../reports/data/2026-11-06-e180-vector-add-aifoundry3/README.md), `c8a3a6ee`, 13:47:01, 91 files): **E180-H NOT RESOLVED, +0.0270 W [−0.0503, +0.1043]**, SIZE FAIL HIGH for P (+0.1649) and C (+0.2197), PASS for E, F and A; **E180-L +0.0097 W a result 1-bit [+0.0027, +0.0167]**, SIZE FAIL HIGH for every arm (P 0.1649, 17×; the lowest arm, A, 0.0192); E180-O NULL (+0.0046 W a bit [−0.0071, +0.0163]); **E180-R PASS**, rho +0.800 (line 0.433); E180-E PASS and PASS; E180-X NOT RESOLVED (−0.0176 W [−0.1892, +0.1541]); **E180-A POSITIVE, +0.3596 W [+0.0683, +0.6510]**, SIZE FAIL HIGH for every arm (P +2.9631), outside P's offset band (+0.752 to +4.261 W); the accumulating sums' walk verified by disassembly (261 PASS, 262 PASS, the control 160 PASS). The owner's sentence for the add: not measured. **Quoted as its dated note 1 corrects the README** ([note 1](../reports/data/2026-11-06-e180-vector-add-aifoundry3/README.md.dated-note-1.md), `210d756e`, 14:49:04, after the data verifier's 1 minor fault and 3 nits): E180-I: NULL within its registered ±0.30 W margin; the eight one-number integer kinds read +0.10 W [+0.04, +0.17] above fadd.pi zeros, above 0 in each of the six passes (P's, C's and E's 0 below the interval; F's +0.041 and A's +0.046 W inside), never "the integer add is flat" (the README's title and the data commit's subject say so) and never "flat" without the margin; the die's rise over a burst, a median of 0.87 °C and a mean of 0.80 °C; the per-kind figures at three decimals as corrected (fadd.ps const +0.067, 3s +0.093 and −4 +0.059 W above zeros; fadd.pi random's P +9.638 W; fadd.pi.acc int 1's and int 4's P +1.198 W). **october-forecast-model's arm, read after the data** ([note 2](../reports/data/2026-11-06-e180-vector-add-aifoundry3/README.md.dated-note-2.md), `b35fb232`, 15:39:57, 4 files, M2's reader run as M2 gives it; reported, never a verdict): his pair's 5s − 4s, +0.0042 W, lies inside E180-H's +0.027 W [−0.050, +0.104], **and so does 0**, so the card cannot tell the model from no difference there; it lies inside on the net minion rail (−0.016 W [−0.077, +0.045]) and for 4s and 5s above the zeros (+0.030 against +0.022 W [−0.099, +0.143]; +0.034 against +0.049 W [−0.060, +0.158]); 0 lies inside all four, and P above all four. The ladder, E180-L, is the sharper comparison, but M1 and M2 give the model no per-bit figure, so it is not read there, and none was derived after the data. M1's figures are consistent with the 00:00:55 frozen sentence at its rounding, never "the same"; the arm is a near-cancellation whose bubble term is labelled assumed. Note 2 answers the first bullet of the README's "Not read here". **Post hoc, the day's critic, deciding nothing:** no carry-over from the burst before (+0.0018 W per W [−0.0026, +0.0062] over 276 bursts), so the 13 low bursts are not made by the run before. The data directory holds 100 files.

**Asked (Q146):** *"adding a matrix filled with 4s is a different than adding a matrix filled with 5s"*. **Registered**
[`4dc5035f`](../../tools/claims-v3/e180/E180.md) (01:22:09 PST Fri 6 Nov; 59 files, `SHA256SUMS-e180-registration` 71): the whole-minion RTL
bench's flips by class with the catalogue loop's gaps (both harts), an accumulating c += v case in `gen_ops.py`, and the watts from the
energy catalogue (`workloads/enercat`, `tools/claims-v3/catfull`, `docs/energy-manual`), frozen before any kit. **Kitted**
[`c92f627b`](../../tools/claims-v3/e180/KIT.md) (01:40:21; 37 files, `SHA256SUMS-e180-kit` 55), V3-CATFULL's block form, 47 runs a pass.
**Amended** by [A1](../../tools/claims-v3/e180/E180.md.dated-note-A1.md) `879197e3` (03:34:44; 17 files, `SHA256SUMS-e180-a1` 41): the vector
arm's rules met as reported arms (the footing labelled; the net minion rail on footing A; the die rise at three fixed slopes), the
registered rules' power at the re-read noise, rule A1-7 for the accumulating sums; and by
[kit note 1](../../tools/claims-v3/e180/KIT.md.dated-note-1.md) with kit v2 `90a1312f` (03:54:42; 41 files, `SHA256SUMS-e180-kit-note1` 90):
D1's other-user rule, E128's telemetry guard, a sync v2 from the harness, the reduction and scorer v2, the build's disassembly walk. No
rule, threshold, plan or forecast changed. **Not run; 0 card-minutes.** Planned: about 43 card-minutes (cap 50), the last of the day's three
runs on aifoundry3. Its build has never been compiled on a box: box step 8 v2 is its first compile, and a failure ends E180's day with no
card time spent.

**The forecasts** (registered, not measured): 5 + 5 = 10.0 is stored as 0x41200000 (three 1-bits) and 4 + 4 = 8.0 as 0x41000000 (two). At
about one result in three the vector unit has a cycle with nothing to write (one hart issues alone at the other's loop end), and in that gap
the result wires (the adder's output, the register write port and three bypass copies) drop to zero, then switch back: 24.995 flips per
result bit per instruction, so 4s flip 50.0 named bits an instruction and 5s 75.0. **His pair: +0.1649 W** at the main price P (±39%; the
other price arms +0.019 to +0.220 W). **The ladder E180-L:** about +0.165 W a result bit; 0.5s (answer 1.0, seven 1-bits) cost +1.15 W over
zeros and 8s (16.0, three) +0.49 W; the 21-number ladder reads the cost of a 1-bit to ±0.003–0.006 W. The integer add is forecast flat (its
result wires hold their value). **E180-A,** c += v: +2.96 W over the plain add, A1's band +0.75 to +4.26 W over the harts' start offset
(SIGN stands; its SIZE and arm reading are not a price test, A1-5). **His pair's rule, as A1 words it:** "POSITIVE 0.37–0.88 if P's price
is right (about 0.75 at the debiased noise, 0.47 under the catalogue pairs' heavy tail), 6 passes; the owner's sentence 0.12–0.78 (about
0.60)". Never 0.88 alone: the ladder E180-L and the ranking E180-R carry the answer.
**Corrections, quoted as corrected:** E180.md section 9's "(E179, Q147's lane, registers them)" becomes "E179 is not registered, built or
run"; section 3's "+4 or +8" becomes "+4 for every step from 2^24 up but the one exact step that crosses it"; sections 4 and 9's "1.4 se
low" becomes 0.25–0.45 se, with C's se 0.16–0.26 pJ; KIT.md's "an idle login does not block (D1)" is true of v2's files only, and its
self-test needs `/usr/bin/python3` with numpy; A1 section 7's "E60's check_elf.py" is E61's (V3-DTF); E180's footing is labelled "the 21 Sep
law; on aifoundry3 within 0.0032 W/C of FL1" (A1-1). `harness-e180-v2.json` does not pin the telemetry reader that E128's guard runs
(E178's and E181's box lists pin it), a records note for a later kit.
